# Event 029 Riches Found decision and mission layer.
#
# This file owns state-targeted actions, mission lifecycles, route-aware AI willingness, concrete
# resource costs, and local outcome flags. The companion trigger/effect files provide the mine
# marker, selected-mine gate, controller reconciliation, and bounded presentation refresh contract.
# No Event 018, biological-outbreak, world-end, or scripted-GUI code is used here.
# The state owner sets riches_found_mine_state and the phase/evolution flags consumed below.

# Decision costs, duration bands, thresholds, and AI willingness factors are shared through
# common/script_constants/029_riches_found_constants.txt.

# ai_hint_pp_cost requires static values; these aliases mirror the pp_* keys
# in riches_found_decision_cost (common/script_constants/029_riches_found_constants.txt).
@RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_LIGHT = 10
@RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD = 25
@RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_HEAVY = 40

# AI scenario coverage is kept beside the willingness blocks: RF_POLICY_01_STABLE_DEMOCRACY,
# RF_POLICY_02_AUTHORITARIAN_MAJOR_AT_WAR, RF_POLICY_03_POOR_MINOR, RF_POLICY_04_COLONIAL_OCCUPIER,
# RF_DEV_01_INLAND_DEEP_REEF, RF_DEV_02_ISLAND_GEMSTONE, RF_DEV_03_COLLAPSE_WARNING,
# RF_REV_01_HIGH_LEGITIMACY_DEMOCRACY, RF_REV_02_WARTIME_FINANCIAL_CRISIS, RF_REV_03_LOW_LEGITIMACY_NON_CORE,
# RF_FOREIGN_01_NEAR_ALLIED_MAJOR, RF_FOREIGN_02_DISTANT_NAVAL_POWER, RF_FOREIGN_03_HOSTILE_RIVAL,
# RF_FOREIGN_04_EXISTING_EXCLUSIVE_CONCESSION, RF_SEC_01_CIVILIAN_CRIME, RF_SEC_02_ARMED_RAID_WARTIME,
# RF_SEC_03_WORKER_CLAIM_WAR, RF_SEC_04_PRIVATE_ENCLAVE, RF_EVO1_01_MANAGED_MINE,
# RF_EVO1_02_CAPTURED_CONCESSION, RF_EVO1_03_MULTIPLE_MINES, RF_EVO2_01_HIGH_PRESSURE_GEMSTONE,
# RF_EVO2_02_SHARED_REVENUE_CONTROLLED_ACCESS, RF_EVO2_03_CLOSED_MINE, RF_EVO3_01_AGGRESSIVE_DEEP_MINE,
# RF_EVO3_02_PARTIALLY_SEALED, RF_EVO3_03_GOLD_DISEASE_MASS_VIOLENCE, RF_ACCOUNT_01_STABLE_STATE,
# RF_ACCOUNT_02_DESPERATE_WAR_STATE, RF_ACCOUNT_03_LOW_STABILITY_HIGH_DEPENDENCE,
# RF_DEMON_01_STABLE_DEMOCRACY_PEACE, RF_DEMON_02_COLLAPSING_AUTHORITARIAN_WAR, and
# RF_DEMON_03_EVACUATION_COMPLETE. These are willingness scenarios, never player-facing probabilities.

riches_found_mine_management = {

decision_riches_found_survey = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_survey
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete }
		}
	}
	available = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		has_political_power > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light
has_manpower > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
has_equipment = { motorized_equipment_1 > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_light }
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_survey
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_LIGHT
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_survey_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_light_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		FROM = {
			set_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_mine_development value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.development_ready compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
	}
}

decision_riches_found_claim_register = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_claim_register
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_registered }
		}
	}
	available = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_registered }
		}
		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_free_mission_slot = yes }
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_registered }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_country_can_start_secure_rush_mission = yes
		has_political_power > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light
has_manpower > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
has_equipment = { support_equipment > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light }
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_claim_register
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_LIGHT
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_claim_register_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light_negative }
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.secure_rush }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.secure_rush_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		FROM = {
			set_state_flag = riches_found_claims_registered
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_7 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_6 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_4 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_illicit_capture = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_extraction_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }
	}
}
decision_riches_found_claim_freeze = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_claim_freeze
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_frozen }
		}
	}
	available = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_frozen }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_frozen }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_country_can_start_secure_rush_mission = yes
		has_political_power > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
command_power > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_light
has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_light }
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_claim_freeze
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.emergency
	fire_only_once = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_claim_freeze_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_light_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_light_negative }
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.secure_rush }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.secure_rush_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		FROM = {
			set_state_flag = riches_found_claims_frozen
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_15 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_5 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_illicit_capture = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_8 }
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = communism }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = fascism }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_extraction_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }
	}
	}

	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_claim_register
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {

			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			has_state_flag = riches_found_local_claims_present
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_local_claims_recognized }
		}
	}
	available = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete

		}
	}

		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {

			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes

			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete

		}

has_manpower > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_recognize_local_claims

	fire_only_once = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_recognize_local_claims_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light_negative
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.secure_rush }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.secure_rush_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		FROM = {
			set_state_flag = riches_found_local_claims_recognized
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_15 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_3 }
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_revenue_legitimacy value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.legitimacy_floor compare = less_than } } }
	}

			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_break_claim_war_subject }

		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_free_mission_slot = yes }
	}
	target_root_trigger = {

			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}

			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_unresolved

		}
	}

		has_political_power > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
has_manpower > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_claims_court

		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative

			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_development
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_development_fund }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_closed }
		}
	}
	available = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_development
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_development_fund }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_closed }
		}
		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_free_mission_slot = yes }
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_development
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_development_fund }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_closed }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_country_can_start_public_settlement_mission = yes
		has_political_power > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
has_manpower > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
has_equipment = { support_equipment > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light }
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_development_fund
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.project
	fire_only_once = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_development_fund_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light_negative }
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.public_settlement }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.public_settlement_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		FROM = {
			set_state_flag = riches_found_development_fund
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_6 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_5 }
			riches_found_record_achievement_public_project = yes
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
	}

			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor has_government = democratic }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor NOT = { is_major = yes } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_allied_target_available } }

			riches_found_limited_contract_can_open_or_extend = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_offtake_agreement }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_exclusive_contract }
		}
	}
	available = {

			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes

		}

		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_foreign
			riches_found_policy_allows_offtake = yes

			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_offtake_agreement }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_exclusive_contract }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		has_political_power > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
has_fuel > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard
has_equipment = { convoy_1 > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.convoy_standard }
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_offtake
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_offtake_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.convoy_standard_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = { riches_found_contract_is_active_and_valid = yes }
				set_state_flag = riches_found_offtake_agreement
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_foreign_claim_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
				riches_found_extend_limited_contract = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_offtake_agreement
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_foreign_claim_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
				set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_contract_type = constant:riches_found_contract_type.offtake }
				riches_found_prepare_foreign_partner = yes
				if = { limit = { has_event_target = riches_found_contract_partner_target } riches_found_begin_contract = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { riches_found_report_kind = constant:riches_found_report.concession }
			riches_found_queue_report = yes
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_allied_target_available } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_war = yes }
	}

						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_active } }
	}
}

	visible = {

			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_bottomless_account

		}

			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_bottomless_account
			has_state_flag = riches_found_account_material_payment_possible
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_material_paid }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		has_political_power > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
has_manpower > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
has_equipment = { support_equipment > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard }
has_fuel > constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_account_pay_material

	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin

	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_account_pay_material_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard_negative }
		add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard_negative
	}
	remove_effect = {
		FROM = {
			set_state_flag = riches_found_account_material_paid
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_supernatural_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_12 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_4 }
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_active } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor has_war = no }
	}

# Multiple mines are managed through one selected state. The selector is
# intentionally in the same ordinary category so it cannot create a second
# category or a parallel scripted-GUI surface.
decision_riches_found_select_mine = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_survey
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			riches_found_mine_is_registered = yes
		}
		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_multiple_mines = yes }
	}
	available = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			riches_found_mine_is_registered = yes
		}
		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_multiple_mines = yes }
	}
	target_root_trigger = { riches_found_country_has_multiple_mines = yes }
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			riches_found_mine_is_registered = yes
		}
	}
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_select_mine_tt
		set_variable = { riches_found_selected_mine = FROM }
		FROM = { set_state_flag = riches_found_selected_target }
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_ai_weight.minimum
		modifier = { add = constant:riches_found_ai_weight.base FROM = { riches_found_mine_is_active = yes } }
		modifier = { add = constant:riches_found_ai_weight.claims FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims } }
	}
}

mission_riches_found_secure_rush = {
	icon = GFX_mission_riches_found_secure_rush
	activation = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	available = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
			has_state_flag = riches_found_camp_administration
			has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_resolved
			check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_floor compare = greater_than }
		}
	}
	cancel_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_effect = {
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_claims_court_open
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	days_mission_timeout = var:riches_found_secure_rush_mission_days
	selectable_mission = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_good = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_secure_rush_complete_tt
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject }
			riches_found_complete_mission = yes
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_success
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
		clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_claims_court_open
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	timeout_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
					has_state_flag = riches_found_camp_administration
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_secure_rush_partial_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_2 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
					set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_partial
					set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
					clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_claims_court_open
				}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_secure_rush_failure_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_12 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_12 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_failure
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_claims_court_open
			}
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress } }
	modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } }
	}
}

}
mission_riches_found_open_railhead = {
	icon = GFX_mission_riches_found_open_railhead
	activation = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	available = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress
			riches_found_state_has_route_capacity = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged }
			check_variable = { var = riches_found_mine_development value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.development_ready compare = greater_than }
		}
	}
	cancel_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_effect = {
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	days_mission_timeout = var:riches_found_open_railhead_mission_days
	selectable_mission = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_good = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_open_railhead_complete_tt
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject }
			riches_found_complete_mission = yes
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_success
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
		clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	timeout_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
					OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_rail_route has_state_flag = riches_found_port_route }
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_open_railhead_partial_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_6 }
					set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_partial
					set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
					clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress
				}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_open_railhead_failure_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_8 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_failure
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress
			}
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } }
	}
	}

			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_progress
			has_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_route_active
			has_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_instance_active
			has_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_protection_started
			has_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_safe_passage_proven
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_route_lost }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_raid_robbery_succeeded }
			riches_found_state_has_supplied_force = yes
			check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_floor compare = greater_than }
		}
	}
	cancel_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_subject
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_effect = {
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_subject }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_route_active
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_instance_active
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_instance_counted
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_protection_started
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_instance_active
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_instance_counted
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_protection_started
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	days_mission_timeout = var:riches_found_protect_pay_train_mission_days
	selectable_mission = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_good = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_protect_pay_train_complete_tt
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_subject }
			riches_found_complete_mission = yes
			riches_found_record_achievement_pay_train = yes
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_success
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
		clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_route_active
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_instance_active
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_instance_counted
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_protection_started
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	timeout_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_subject
					has_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_route_active
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_protect_pay_train_partial_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_2 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
					set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_partial
					set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
					clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_route_active

				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_subject }

					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_failure
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_route_active
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_instance_active
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_instance_counted
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_pay_train_protection_started
			}
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}

		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_protect_pay_train_progress } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } }

				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_8 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_success
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
		clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_progress
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	timeout_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_subject
					OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_public_project_complete has_state_flag = riches_found_development_fund }
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_public_settlement_partial_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
					set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_partial
					set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
					clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_progress
				}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_public_settlement_failure_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_10 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_10 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_12 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_failure
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_progress
			}
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_progress } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } }
	}
}

mission_riches_found_audit_concession = {
	icon = GFX_mission_riches_found_audit_concession
	activation = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	available = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_progress
			has_state_flag = riches_found_audit_complete
			has_state_flag = riches_found_beneficial_owners_disclosed
			has_state_flag = riches_found_audit_records_recovered
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_records_destroyed }
		}
	}
	cancel_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_effect = {
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_progress
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	days_mission_timeout = var:riches_found_audit_concession_mission_days
	selectable_mission = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_good = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_audit_concession_complete_tt
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject }
			riches_found_complete_mission = yes
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_illicit_capture = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_15 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_foreign_claim_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_6 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_success
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
		clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_progress
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	timeout_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject
					OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_beneficial_owners_disclosed has_state_flag = riches_found_audit_records_recovered }
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_audit_concession_partial_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_2 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_illicit_capture = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_6 }
					set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_partial
					set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
					clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_progress
				}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_audit_concession_failure_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_12 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_illicit_capture = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_15 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_foreign_claim_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_failure
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_cooldown days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_progress
			}
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_audit_concession_progress } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } }
	}
}

mission_riches_found_evacuate_workforce = {
	icon = GFX_mission_riches_found_evacuate_workforce
	activation = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	available = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_mine_is_active = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_ready
			has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
			OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_rail_route has_state_flag = riches_found_port_route }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_severe_collapse }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_preparation_pending }
			check_variable = {
				var = global.date
				value = riches_found_demons_evacuation_completion_date
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_effect = {
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_authorized
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_ready
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
			clear_variable = riches_found_demons_evacuation_completion_date
			set_state_flag = riches_found_achievement_last_shift_invalidated
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	days_mission_timeout = constant:riches_found_duration.evacuation_mission_days
	selectable_mission = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_good = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_evacuate_workforce_complete_tt
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_demons_district_evacuated
			set_state_flag = riches_found_workforce_evacuated
			set_state_flag = riches_found_achievement_evacuation_route_valid
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
			set_variable = { riches_found_last_shift_controller = CONTROLLER }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_supernatural_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_20 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_15 }
			riches_found_complete_mission = yes
			riches_found_record_achievement_evacuation = yes
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_authorized
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_ready
			clear_variable = riches_found_demons_evacuation_completion_date
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	timeout_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
					has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_evacuate_workforce_partial_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
				set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_partial
				set_state_flag = riches_found_achievement_last_shift_invalidated
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_authorized
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_ready
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
				clear_variable = riches_found_demons_evacuation_completion_date
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_evacuate_workforce_failure_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
				set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_failure
				set_state_flag = riches_found_achievement_last_shift_invalidated
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_authorized
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_ready
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
				clear_variable = riches_found_demons_evacuation_completion_date
			}
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_supernatural_crisis } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_severe_collapse } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
	}
}
